#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1C_eventPicture
	}

	trigger = {
	NOT = {

			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}

	trigger = {

			which = cosmoHaremVisit

			}
		}
		}

	}

		}

	}

			}
	}

	}

	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}

		value = 1
		}
		}

		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_1

				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_africawives_menu_8						
		}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
	OR = {
		culture_group = mande		
		culture_group = sahelian		
		culture_group = west_african		
		culture_group = southern_african		
		culture_group = kongo_group		
		culture_group = great_lakes_group		
		culture_group = african		
		culture_group = cushitic		
		culture_group = sudanese		
	}		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_wives_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_nawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant harem 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = beja
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = beja
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = beja
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = beja
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = beja
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = beja
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = beja
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = beja
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant harem 2a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.21
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.21.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.21.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_2A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_2a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = amhara
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = amhara
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = amhara
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_2a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_2a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = amhara
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = amhara
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = amhara
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_2a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_2a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.21.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = amhara
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = amhara
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_2a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.21.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant harem 3a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.31
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.31.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.31.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_3A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_3a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = afar
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = afar
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = afar
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_3a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_3a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = afar
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = afar
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = afar
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_3a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.31.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = afar
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = afar
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_3a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.31.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}

			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_4a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = madagasque
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = madagasque
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = madagasque
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_4a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_4a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = madagasque
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = madagasque
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = madagasque
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_4a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_4a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.41.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = madagasque
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = madagasque
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_4a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.41.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_5b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_5b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = masaba
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = masaba
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = masaba
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_5b
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_5b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.52.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = masaba
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = masaba
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort

		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

			duration = 3650

			duration = 3650

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			duration = 3650

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA

#grant harem 5c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.53
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.53.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.53.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_5C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_5c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = rwandan
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = rwandan
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = rwandan
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#			days = 30

#grant harem 6c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.63
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.63.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.63.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_6C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_6c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = shona
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = shona
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = shona
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_6c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_6c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shona
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shona
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shona
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_6c
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_6c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

#grant harem 7c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.73
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.73.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.73.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_7C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_7c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = lunda
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = lunda
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = lunda
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_7c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_7c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lunda
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lunda
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lunda
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_7c
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_7c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.73.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = lunda
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = lunda
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_7c								
		country_event = {

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

#grant harem 8c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.83
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.83.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.83.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_8C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_8c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yaka
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yaka
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = yaka
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_8c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_8c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yaka
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yaka
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yaka
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_8c
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_8c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.83.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = yaka
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = yaka
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_8c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.83.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

#grant harem 10a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1001
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1001.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_10A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_10a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = aka
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = aka
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = aka
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_10a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_10a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aka
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aka
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aka
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_10a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_10a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1001.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = aka
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = aka
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_10a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1001.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA

	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1011
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1011.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1011.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_11A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = fon
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = fon
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = fon
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = fon
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = fon
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = fon
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_11a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_11a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1011.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = fon
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = fon
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_11a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1011.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = bozo
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bozo
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bozo
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = bozo
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_12a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_12a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {

			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}

				OR = {
				primary_culture = bozo
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}

			add_province_modifier = {

				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_13a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_13a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1031.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = bambara
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {

			}	
			}

		country_event = {

			}

#grant harem 13b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1032
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1032.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1032.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_13B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = songhai
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = songhai
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = songhai
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = songhai
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = songhai
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = songhai
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_13b
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_13b_DESC
				}

			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_14a
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_14a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1041.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = bilala
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = bilala
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_14a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}

#grant harem 14b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1042
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1042.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1042.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_14B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hausa
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hausa
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hausa
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hausa
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hausa
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hausa
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_14b
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_14b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1042.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hausa
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = hausa
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_14b

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {

		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant harem 15b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1052
	title = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1052.t
	desc = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1052.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_15B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = senegambian
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 5
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = senegambian
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = senegambian
				}
		}
				NOT = {
					has_estate_influence_modifier = {
						estate = estate_harem
						modifier = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
					}
				}			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = senegambian
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = senegambian
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = senegambian
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = africa_concubine_15b
				duration =  -1
				desc = africa_concubine_15b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1052.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = senegambian
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = senegambian
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_africawives_15b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_africamarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1052.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_AFRICA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

	NOT = {

			}

			}

	}

		clr_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoHaremVisit
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit	
			value = 140
			}
			}

		value = 0
			}
		}
		}			
		}

			}

			}
			}

			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			}

			}

	}	
	}

					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_11c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																					
		}				
}

		   }

				value = 1
				}

		change_variable = {

		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesEightBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor

		}

						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																					
		}

					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {

					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
			}					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1500
			}
			}																		
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_13b

					}
				}				
				any_province = {

			value = 280
			}
			}

			}
		}
	}

			}

			}

			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt

			}
		}

		}

					}
				}

				any_province = {

					OR = {

			}

			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1420
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1400.c
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14c
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14c
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1430
			}
		}
		}		
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1400.7
			if = {
				limit = {					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1500
			}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_15c
					}
				}

				}

				which = cosmoAfricanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}

		}

		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFourteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_africaese_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFourteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFourteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_14B_1_eventPicture

		ai = no
	
	}

		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africawivesestate_events.1420.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoAfricanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {

			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter
